5a1(1): @#/ O /hU~M hU~M hU~M/de nas yab yum gnyis su med pa las/khro bo dang khro mo dang /sprul pa dang /yang sprul du bcas pa thams cad thon par gyur nas/hU~M hU~M hU~M/
5a2(1): phaT phaT phaT badzra ki la ya hU~M phaT/ces pa rdo rje phur pa drag po'i sgra nas/'jig rten dregs pa'i rba skongs nas/yang rang bzhin gyis gnyis su med par mnyam pa nyid kyis bzhugs 
5a3(1): te|sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyis bzhugs so////rdo rje khros pa rtsa ba'i rgyud las/gleng bslang ba'i le'u ste/gnyis pa'o//
5a4(1): //de ltar mnyam pa nyid la gnas pa la/bdag tu rmongs pa dang /rtags pa la mngon par zhen pa'i dbang gis/yang dag pa'i lam dang bral 
5a5(1): nas|gab pa'i gsang ba ni ma rtogs par/sbas pas gsang ba la mngon par bzhen nas/rgyu dang 'bras bu la rmongs nas/srid pa'i sa bon gtsub mor gyur pa nas/tsha 
5a6(1): ba'i dmyal bar skyes so//tsha ba'i sdug bsngal gyis brdungs pas//shin tu grang ba'i nang du skyes/tsha ba dang grang ba dang 'jig rten gyi khams rgyud cing /bskal pa stong  
5a7(1): phrag bcu gnyis su myong ngo //de nas yan lag sna tshogs pa dang /gdug pa'i lag cha sna tshogs pa dang /nad bzhi brgya rtsa bzhi(sa) 'debs par byed la/lha'i rigs dang lha  
5a8(1): ma yin dang /tshangs pa dang 'bras bu che ba man chad dbang du bsdus so//bde bar gshegs pa thams cad mkhyen pas gzigs nas/thugs rje  gyis brlabs kyis/
5b1(1): bde bar gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa byin gyis brlabs kyis/de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje gzhon nu bde bar gshegs pa thams cad kyis sras su bskyed nas/
5b2(1): sras ni chos nyid skye ba med pa las//'jigs pa'i gzugs skur bzhengs pa'i phyir na sras//mchog ni de las bzhengs kyang g.yo' ba med//sras ni ru Ta 'dul phyir mi bzad phur 
5b3(1): pa drug/ces bde bar gshegs byin gyis brlabs kyi nas//dbu gsum phyag zhabs sprul pa'i phra men ma/byi la'i 'go can dang /phug shud kyi 'go can no//oM badzra kro ha a par tsi ta hU~M/
5b4(1): ti sta ti sta hU~M phaT/nub byang du khro bo chen po gzhan gyis mi thub pa dang /yum rdo rje mda' bsnyems ma/sprul pa phra men ma/sprung ku'i mgo can/khra'i mgo can no//A+oM badzra kro ha a myi 
5b5(1): ta kun d+ha li hU~M/tshin d+ha tshin d+ha hU~M phaT/byang du khro bo chen po bdud rtsi 'khyil ba dang /sprul pa'i phra men ma seng ge'i mgo can dang /pha wang gi mgo can no//A+oM badzra kro ha tre'i lo kya bi tsa 
5b6(1): ya hU~M|bin d+ha bin d+ha hU~M phaT/byang shar du khro bo rnam par rgyal ba dang /yum rdo rje gsod byed ma chen mo/sprul pa'i phra men ma tred kyi mgo can dang /sre mo'i mgo can nor/A+oM badzra 
5b7(1): kro ta ma hA pa la hU~M phaT/spo he spo he hU~M phaT/'og tu khro bo stobs po che dang /yum rdo rje bskyod byed ma/sprul pa'i phra men dom gyi mgo can dang /byi ba'i mgo can gyis gtams 
5b8(1): pa'o|/de rnams thams cad kyang thon par gyur te/rab tu khros par gyur to//A+oM badzra ki li ki la ya hU~M hU~M hU~M phaT phaT phaT/ces rab tu 'khos te/stong gsum dus gcig   
6a1(1): @#/ O /g.yos so//srin po chen po gdug pa dang bcas pa bros so//mi thub par 'chor ba las bcas ldan Irdo rje gzhon nu nyid kyis/shin 
6a2(1): tu khros rab tu 'khros pa las/mi bzod pa'i sras mchog rnam pa drug mthon par gyur te/ta tha ya ta tha ya hU~M phaT/ha sa ya ha na hU~M phaT/shi kri na han hU~M phaT/bi da ya  
6a3(1): bi da ya hU~M phaT/ces sras mchog rnam pa drug gis/rlong chen drug gis las bya ste//stod ni zhal gsum phyag drug pa//smad ni zur gsum dbal dang ldan//khro bo 
6a4(1): bcu yis yongs brgyan nas//gdug pa ma lus 'dul bar byos//snying rjes bsgral bas dam tshig ni//gsad cing mnan pa ma yin te//phung por rdo rje rtams byas nas 
6a5(1): rnam par shes pa rdo rjer bsgom//hU~M hU~M hU~M ki li ki la ya ces rab tu 'khros pas/rang dbang chen por bskur te/rdo rje gzhon nus rigs rnams//srid pa rdo rje grub mdzod cig/
6a6(1): srid pa rdo rje phur pa'i lha/ye shes khro bo grub par mdzod//sangs rgyas kun gyi ye shes sku//nga nyid rdo rje chos dbyings las//'bar ba'i bzhi pa gcig tu sprul nas/
6a7(1): hU~M hU~M hU~M|phaT phaT phaT/ces 'khros (pa) lus po cig la mgo brgyad par sprul/phyag na mtshon cha sna tshogs bsnams pa/'khor thams cad kyang bsdus nas/shin tu    
6a8(1): 'gying bag dang bcas par gyur to//hi hi hi zhes shin tu nga rgyal dang bcas par gyur to/ /de nas bcom ldan 'das sras mchog nyid kyis/dur khrod kyi rlong na gdug pa can gyi gdan 
6b1(1): la bzhugs nas/A+o~M ki li ki la ya sarnga big nan baM hU~M phaT/ces brjod pas rngam brjid dang ldan par gyur to//g.yas dkar g.yon dmar dbus mthing ba/zhal gsum 
6b2(1): mche ba bcu gnyis pa/shangs gsum pa ba spu gyen du 'khyil ba/phyag drug dang po na rdo rje rtse dgu pa/bar pa rtse lnga pa/g.yon gyi dang po na me dpung /bar pa na kha Ta~M rtse gsum pa /
6b3(1): tha ma gnyis kyis rdo rje phur pa 'gril ba/dur khrod kyi chas brgyad gsel ba/rdzu 'phrul gyi zhabs bzhis bzhugs pa/rdo rje gshog pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams khyengs pa/sku mchog 
6b4(1): gang ba'i lus la gtams pa/yum dang gnyis su med par gyur to////rdo rje 'khros pa zhe sdang gcod//mtshon cha sngon po 'bar ba yis//nam mkha'i dkyil
6b5(1): nas thigs pa shar//srog gi go ru shar ba dang /snying gi go ru bsgom par bya/ces gsungs so////de nas khro bo'i tshogs thams cad sprul pa las/
6b6(1): A+o~M badzra kro ha hU~M ka ra gar dza gar dza hU~M phaT/ces steng du khro bo chen po hU~M ka ra/yum rdo rje sgra 'byin ma/sprul pa'i 'phra men ma/phag gi 'go can dang /smig bu'i mgo can no//A+o~M 
6b7(1): kro ha bi dza ya hU~M/ha na ha na hU~M phaT/shar du khro bo chen po rnam par rgyal ba dang /yum rdo rje rnam par bsnyems ma/sprul pa'i 'phra men na/stag gi mgo can dang /bya rgod kyi 'go   
6b8(1): can no//A+o~M kro ha ni la ha na tha hU~M phaT/d+ha ha d+ha ha hU~M phaT/shar lho'i mtshams su khro bo chen po dbyug pa sngon po can dang /yum rdo rje ser mo la/sprul pa'i 'phra men ma/
8a1(1): @#/ O /sha zos khrag 'chos rus pa kun 'chos nas/hU~M hU~M hU~M phaT phaT phaT/ces brjod pa bden pa'i don Ia su 'chi 'bangs su 'chi/
8a2(1): 'bangs su mchil nan tan ma chungs cig/shes pa bla myed gnas stabs nas//phung po dur khrod gnas bya zhing /gzugs phung nga'i gdan bya'o//chos nyid kyi bden pa'o//
8a3(1): gsang sngags kyis byin gyis brlabs so//sangs kyi mthu'o//a bi ra tsa'i dus la bab po//gnag pa de kho na la'o//ces byin gyis brlabs kyis snang ngo //rdo rje 'khros 
8a4(1): pa rtsa brgyud las/drag po dregs pa can gtul pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas dregs pa 'dul ba'i rtso bo//'jig   
8a5(1): rten gyi dregs pa pho mo rnams sdangs nas/ye shes dang las la grub pa'i bud myed dkar mo shel gyi lan phran can gyis kyang /srog gi snying po phul nas/dpa' bo chen po 
8a6(1): 'khor bcas la//bdag cag rnams kyang 'bangs su mchi//yang bud myed nag mo khrag gi ral pa can gyis kyang /bdag gi srog gi snying po bzhengs su gsol//de yang bud 
8a7(1): myed dmar mo byi ru'i rdang ral can gyis/dpa' bo chen po'i spyan lam du//bdag gi srog snying dam pa 'di/dpa' bo khyod kyi bzhes su gsol//a tsi ha a pa ra tsi 
8a8(1): te|dza ya bi dza ye/ka ta~M ka he ye/mar sa na bra mar rta ni ye hU~M phaT/bdag cag rnams kyi srog snying 'di//dpa' bo chen pos bzhes su gsol//sku gsung thugs 
8b1(1): las rdzogs pa sngags/dpa' bo khyod la 'bul bar bgyi/ces srog gis snying po phul nas/khas blangs dam bcas las/rdo rje gsang ba'i bka' las ni//sdig can 
8b2(1): gang zhig 'da' byed pa//khro chen khro bo chen po yis//glad pa tshal ba brgya ru khos//nyon cig bgegs dang log 'dren tshogs//nga yi bka' las 'da' ma byed//ces gnad 
8b3(1): la bor bas/yang srog gi snying po phul te//dpa' bo thugs kyi dkyil 'khor du//bdag cag 'khor dang bcas pa yi//srog gi snying po 'bul bar bgyi//bka'i pha 
8b4(1): 'bag gsal du gsol//hU~M ma ma bshing ku ru/ma ha~M nya ka ra a nan/d+ha hi ma ma kar ma/shi kraM ka re ya hU~M phaT/dpa' bo chen po'i spyan lam du//srog snying drag sngags 
8b5(1): 'di dbul gyis//thugs rje spyan gyis gzigs su gsol//ces phul lo//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i//'khor 'das bcas pa ma kun//dam chos 'di la  
8b6(1): brten pa yis//ma lus las rnam grub par gyis//dam tshig chen po'i dus la bab//sprul pa chen po'i dus la bab/bdag nyid chen mo'i dus la bab//sho na mu kha'i 
8b7(1): dus la bab/sa bdag chen mo'i dus la bab/khyod kyi thugs dam dus la bab//mthu rtsal byung ba'i dus la bab//ces nad la bor nas dam rdzas la brten cing bran gyi 
8b8(1): tshul du khas blangs so//ston pa chen po sangs rgyas gtso//drag po chen po 'jigs pa'i gzugs//mnyam pa nyid kyis ston pa po rig/zhi ba'i sems can  
10a1(1): ja
10a2(1): @#/ O /pa dang /sdug bsngal bskyed pa la sogs bya//rdungs cig rdo rje 'bar ba'i brtun//bka' las 'da' byed /rdo rje me dbal snying bsregs   
10a3(1): nas//lus ngag rdul phran bzhin du glogs//khro mo 'bar ba'i rtun khung du//rdo rje tho ba'i brdung bar bya//lha yang rung ste brlag par 'gyur//kha tham kha tham kha tham phaT phaT phaT/
10a4(1): badzra yag sha kro ha kha hi kha hi/ha ha ha/yab yum bar du brdung bya zhing //dngos po sha khrag lha yis sku//stabs pa'i dgyes pas rta rgad 'gyur//nyi shu rtsa gcig sngags rnams kyis//
10a5(1): dkar nag 'tshams su gdab par bya//hU~M hU~M hU~M phaT phaT phaT/rdo rje 'khros pa rtsa ba'i rgyud las/dgegs btul ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o////
10a6(1): de nas mnyam pa'i ting nge 'dzin la bzhugs nas/dmyigs pa myed pa'i ting nge 'dzin 'dis/ting nge 'dzin rgyal por bstan par gyur//ma nor ma bcos dbyings nyid las//rang bzhin  
10a7(1): lhun gyis grub pa'i dbyings//dngos myed gsal ba'i ye shes la//de la sems kyi 'bras bu bskyed//thog mar gzhan yang byang chub sems//de ni rgyu 'bras ma bkag chos//
10a8(1): de nas bdag gi byang chub sems//srid pa gsum la dbang bsgyur zhing /ba ga ban dang skal pa gcig/byang chub sems ni mngon du bstan/khams sems na mngon du bsan//khams   
10a9(1): gsum ye shes bskyed pa'i phyir//bsgrub pa'i ye shes mtshon gang po//gsad pa'i sngags kyi bskyed nas kyang //sku dang ldan par rab brgyan cing //khams gsum khro bos gang bar mdzod/
10b1(1): gnyis ldan sku gsung thugs su ldan//shin tu brjid pa'i ye shes kyis//sku'i tshig gis rab brgyan cing //thugs la 'bar ba'i srog kyang bzhugs//dbu la rdzogs pa'i rigs lnga 
10b2(1): bsrung //nya zla'i ri rab phur bur brgyan//khro bo mang po gzir mnan nas//yum ni rnam par grol ba'i thugs//srid pa'i phur bu rtogs pa'i ngang //srid pa gsum yang thebs zhes bya//
10b3(1): khams gsum gdul bsgral ba'i phyir//mtshan ma gzi brjid glog ltar 'bar//me dpung gsum gyis khams gsum bsreg/nyes byed gsum yang srid par bsgrol//khams gsum 
10b4(1): dus gcig dbang bskur nas//rab tu srog gi gor shar ba//gnyis ldan sku gsung thugs kun ldan//dus cig leb 'bar bar smin//rdzogs na dud kha gsang phyir ro//mthil la gnyis 
10b5(1): zla'i dkyil 'khor bsgom//lag tu blangs te dril nas kyang //khams gsum du kha bsgral bar gyur//yum bzhi phyag rgya'i byin rlabs zhing //de nas a las rnam par dag/srid pa gsum 
10b6(1): du gsal 'tsher nas//'khor ba gsum ni mya ngan 'das//'di ni byang chub sems kyi 'khrus//byang chub sems kyi khyad par las//khro bo'i phur bu'i rab brgyan cig/khams 
10b7(1): gsum dbang bar yang dag grub//des na yul sems gnyis su med//skye ba myed pa'i don rab gyis//kha'i rtun khung bde gshegs gnas//gting dpag myed pa'i dbyings nyid 
10b8(1): du//lha yang rung ste myos brgyal nas//srid pa'i phur bu thebs zhes bya//khro mo 'bar ba'i rtun khung du//khro bo 'bar ba'i rtun tshogs kyis//byang chub sems kyi 'phro 'du yis//
11a1(1): khams gsum ma lus bsgral zhes bya//rnam par shes pa ye shes mchog/srog dang spu gos bya ba dang //byang chub sems kyi phur pa 
11a2(1): las//mkhas pa bzhin du lhun gyis grub//mnyam pa'i rgyal pos yang dag bsgral//rtog 'dzin med pa'i dbang phyug des//dpa' bo chen po 'dul ba bzhin//shin tu bsgral te 
11a3(1): dgyes btang nas//za byed kun gyis stobs par mdzod//ha ha bgyes pas dgyes par mdzod//phaT phaT kyis ni 'khor lo bri//hU~M hU~M bgyis pas dbang du bsdud//kha hi bgyis pas lhag ma 
11a4(1): bzhes//rdo rje rab tu ki la ya//rab tu gsor te dril nas kyang //khams gsum 'khor bar bsgral bar bya//sems kyi yul rnams bsgral nas ni//ye shes sku'i khyab brdal nas//
11a5(1): 'dzin chags thams cad rnam par grol//de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mthu//byang chub sems las 'od 'phros pas//mkhas pa de bzhin rol pa'i sku//ma lus phur bu thebs zhes bya//
11a6(1): gang zhig gnas pa'i phyogs rnams la//rnam par shes pa'i tshogs brgyad gnas//rdo rje phur pa ma shar shing //de'i tshogs brgyad dag par bya//bdag dang gsum du ldan nas kyang //
11a7(1): byang chub sems la dbang spros te//phung po rdul du bshig nas kyang //sha khrag khams gsum gang bar dmigs//hU~M gis rab tu nam mkha' gang //phaT kyis kun tu gtor bya ste//rdo rje 
11a8(1): ye shes mtshon gang gis//khams gsum rab tu bkang nas kyang //ho yis rab tu dag pa yis//snying po 'di skad ces brjod do//rab rgyud mchog sems rab tu gsal ba'i phyir//
11b1(1): mnyam nyid rig rtsal las rang nyid grol//de bzhin hU~M gis drangs nas ni//phung po gzi ldan thob nas kyang //bsdus pa'i thig le chen por bsgom//khams gsum byang chub sems bskyed 
11b2(1): do//'gro ba ma lus don bya'i phyir//spros pa ljang kun nam mkha' khyab//dmar po srog gi thugs kyang lags//ljang kun las kyi sa bon te//mthing ka he ru ka'i thugs//
11b3(1): thams cad kun tu brjod 'grub pas//de nges sems kyi phung po 'tshol//khams gsum dur khrod khrag yin te//thugs rjes chags pa bsgral ba'i phyir//srid pa brdar sha bcad nas ni//
11b4(1): spyod pa thams bcud dang ldan//hU~M zhes bya ba skal bzang ngo //srid pa dbang du bsdus nas ni//khams gsum kun las 'das par bsgom//'jig rten kun las 'das par bsgom //'jig rten 
11b5(1): kun las 'das pas na//lhan ne bo dang bdag nyid sbyor//'jig rten kun las grub pa'i rgyal//man ngag nying khu gsang ba'i rjes//'dzin dang rtog pa rnam spangs te//byang chub sems 
11b6(1): ni skad cig ma//snang dang srid dang 'dzin rtog dang //bsam pa las 'das mya ngan 'das//'jig rten pa las mya ngan 'das//byang chub sems kyi grol sa gcad//grub mtha' 'di la 
11b7(1): 'jig mtha' myed//'di ni myi 'gyur byang chub sems//mya ngan 'das pa rtogs pa mo//sgrib pa med pa mtha' phyin pa//byas pa myed par lhun gyis grub//myi nus pa la rtog byas shing //
11b8(1): rdo rje sems dpa'i mtha' la nub//nub pa med pa shin tu spags//'di ni snyan gyi bu ga nas//snyan gyi bu gar gsang ba yin//rgya mtsho zhig ring mi dor ba bzhin//byang chub sems ni rdzogs 
12a1(1): @#//
12b1(1): kyi phur pa 'di//rtogs tshogs 'dul ba'i phyir yum gyi mkha' la brdab//'dus byas rdzas kyi phur pa 'dis//bsgral ba'i zhing bcur gyur pa rnams la gdab//'dus byas rdzas
12b2(1): kyi phur pa la//man ngag nyid du bkod pa bdun//med du mi rung de bzhin bsam snyen phur rnam pa gnyis yin te//nyungs kar dang ni phur bu dngos//lcags sna lnga 'am bdun kyang rung //
12b3(1): 'go brgya mdung rtse zur gsum pa//rked pa rgya mdud bzhal yas khang //lcim bzhog brgyad la sor bcu gnyis//snyen pa'i phur par de bzhin bya//seng ldeng phur bu sor brgyad ni//
12b4(1): mthu 'phrog byin brlabs yod pa yin//de bzhin bya rgod gshog phur yang //'gugs par byed pa'i nus pa yod//ser po gser ram yang na ni//skyer pa dag la byas pa yis//
12b5(1): zil gyis mnan pa'i phur pa yin//skyes pa dar ma'i rigs can gyi//sbom pa ma yin 'phra mo 'am//zangs sam ljon dmar shing dag gam//dmar po dag la byas pa 'di//
12b6(1): dbang du bsdud pas phur pa bstan//tsan dan dag gam rgya shug gi/gzi byin 'phrog par yang dag bstan//lcags sam shi shing nag po 'am//drag por gyur pa mtha' dag la//
12b7(1): gdul du klog par rab tu bstan//phyogs kyi khro bo bcu po la//bcu mtshan bcu 'am yang na bcu na bcu//de bzhin mang nyung grangs kyis dbye//mthing nag dar gyi gcod paN gdags//
12b8(1): lha 'am rdzas su spyod kyang rung //mtshan dang ldan pa'i ban da ru//nag po 'bru yi bkang nas ni//rim par shugs shing byin gyis brlabs//mthing nag dar gyi kha gcad nas//
13a1(1): @#//byin brlabs dbang bskur gzhung bzhin bya//mi 'gyur pho brang rdzas su bkod//me lha tha ma dang //nga la ser//drag po'i hom thab  
13a2(1): gzhung bzhin bya//tsher ma can gyi shing btsugs la//ming dang rus su bcas pa dang //drag po rtsang gis bskor bar bya//bsreg pa'i rdzas rnams kun kyang bzhag/lho  
13a3(1): nub srin po mnan pa'i sar//de bzhin drag po gru gsum la//rim pa gsum du bskor//mon nam rabs chad thod pa la//gang yin ming rus bris nas ni//mtha' ma drag po'i 
13a4(1): rtsang gis bskor//gru gsum dbus kyi steng du gzhag/log pa med pa mthar phyin pa'i//brgya dang brgyad kyis phur pas bskor//mtha' ma de bzhin rtsang gis bskor//
13a5(1): byang nub rlung lha 'phang dbye ba'i sar//drag po gru gsum gong bzhin bya//thod pa dum bu drug pa 'am//mtshan ma ngan par gyur pa yis//bri ba'i thabs ni gang yin bri//gang yin 
13a6(1): ming rus bcu bcug nas ni//drag po gru gsum dbus su bzhag/man ngag nyid du bkod pa yi//phur bu zur gsum dbal dang ldan//drag po'i gtor ma steng du bkod//khrag sna lnga dang 
13a7(1): snying sna lnga//mtshe nag nyungs nag (de) mda'//drag po zor gyi rdzas su bzhag/mtha' ma de bzhin rtsang (   )gis bskor//byang shar 'byung po dbang ldan sgrol ba'i sar//gru gsum rim  
13a8(1): pa gong bzhin bya//bsgral ba'i rim pa gang yin pa//snying kar rus pa dpral bar ming //yan lag bzhi la yi ge bzhi//che ge mo ni dbus su gzhug/ma ra ya yi mtha' ma bskor/
13b1(1): gru gsum dbus su gzhug par bya//mtha' ma rtsang gis de bzhin bskor//dbus kyi pho brang dgod pa ni//ling ka bya ba'i rgyu rnams la//gzugs dang ling ka gzhung bzhin bya//
13b2(1): rno ba mtshon gyis lus la btur//lcag thag nag pos mgul nas btags//yan lag lnga mtshon skud lnga//de bzhin pho brang dbus su bkod//tha ma rtsang gis de bzhin bskoro//
13b3(1): bsnyen pa'i pur pas gzir la gzhag/de'i phyi rim gru gsum la//khro bcu phur pa rim pa bzhin//sras mchog mi bzad phur bu drug/sgo bzhi steng 'og gnyis dag gam//yang na   
13b4(1): rim pa'i sngon du bkod//dpal gyi gtor ma gzhung bzhin bshams//srungs ma rnam gsum mthun rdzas bsag/drag po'i las su gyur pa yis//zangs lcags rdo rje khrag dang dug 
13b5(1): khyab par bal mtshon byungs kyis dag pa//srungs ma rnam gsum phyag mtshan dang //rtun dang rdo rje tho ba dang /thun rdzas sna tshogs dgod par bya//rdo rje 'khros pa rtsa ba'i rgyud las drag

15b7(1): A+o~M la~M hU~M la~M/stam pa nan/mo hA g+ha ya/ba ka ban/shi kri bi kri/badzra hU~M ka ra hU~M hU~M phaT phaT/A+o~M badzra ki li ki la ya/dza hU~M ba~M ho/sarba big nan/badzra ki li ki la ya hU~M hU~M phaT
